## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this machine. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you keep working on something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들면 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 좋아요. 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先する通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認し、順番どおりに再送して渡すので確実に届きます。UDPは確認も再送もせず、速く軽く送るだけなので、遅延に弱い動画通話やオンラインゲームなどでよく使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Rebase reescribe tus commits sobre una base actualizada para dejar una historia lineal; úsalo en ramas tuyas y privadas, antes de compartirlas. Merge conserva la historia tal cual y une dos ramas con un commit de unión, así que es la opción segura para ramas ya publicadas o compartidas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、保证可靠有序送达的；UDP 不建连接、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is your own and hasn't been shared, so you can tidy it into a clean line before a PR. Merge when others already have the branch, because rebasing rewrites history and breaks their copies.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records each change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes midway, it can replay or undo those logged entries on restart, so committed data survives and nothing is left half-written.
```

```
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五的 G1、G3 都是 553 元，已经放进看板，每天早上更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1792 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海，600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">2 班</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">车次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有车次数据</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function render(trains) {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    trains = Array.isArray(trains) ? trains : [];
    var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
    count.textContent = under.length ? under.length + ' 班' : '没有';
    sub.textContent = trains.length ? '共看了 ' + trains.length + ' 班，每天早上更新' : '每天早上更新';
    empty.hidden = trains.length > 0;
    trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no + '（二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张）';
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
      if (t.price < 600) val.style.color = 'var(--d-good)';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined') ducter.summary(under.length ? under.length + ' 班 600 以下' : '暂无');
  }

  if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined') {
    ducter.get('trains').then(render);
    ducter.onChange(function () { ducter.get('trains').then(render); });
  } else {
    render([]);
  }
```

```
}
```

```
  var name = $('item').value.trim();
  if (editId) {
    items = items.map(function (it) {
      return it.id === editId ? { id: it.id, day: day, item: name, amount: amount } : it;
    });
  } else {
    items.push({ id: uid(), day: day, item: name, amount: amount });
  }
  await persist();
  closeForm();
  render();
});

$('del').addEventListener('click', async function () {
  if (!editId) return;
  items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.id !== editId; });
  await persist();
  closeForm();
  render();
});

$('cancel').addEventListener('click', closeForm);

ducter.onChange(function () { load(); });
resetForm();
load();
```

```
<div id="del"></div>

<script>
(function(){
  var CATS=['Food','Transport','Shopping','Bills','Other'];
  var items=[], cat='Food', sel=null;
  function $(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
  function fmt(n){return n.toFixed(2);}
  function el(tag,cls,text){var e=doc … row.appendChild(el('div','k-row-value', fmt(e.amount)));
      row.onclick=function(){ sel=e.id; showDel(); };
      list.appendChild(row);
    });

    ducter.summary(items.length ? (month.length+' this month, '+fmt(total)) : 'Nothing yet');
    showDel();
  }

  function showDel(){
    var box=$('del'); box.innerHTML='';
    if(sel==null) return;
    var e=items.filter(function(x){return x.id===sel;})[0];
    if(!e){ sel=null; return; }
    var b=el('button','k-btn danger','Delete "'+e.note+'" ('+fmt(e.amount)+')');
    b.onclick=function(){
      items=items.filter(function(x){return x.id!==sel;});
      sel=null; save(); render();
    };
    box.appendChild(b);
  }

  $('add').onclick=function(){
    var a=parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if(!(a>0)) return;
    items.push({id:Date.now(), amount:a, note:$('note').value.trim()||cat, cat:cat, at:new Date().toISOString()});
    save();
    $('amt').value=''; $('note').value='';
    $('addBox').open=false;
    render();
  };

  function load(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(function(v){
      items=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];
      render();
    });
  }

  renderCats();
  load();
  ducter.onChange(load);
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
</section>

<h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>

<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-chips" id="chips"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="120"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>时间（空着就是现在，如 14:30）</label><input class="k-input" id="tm" placeholder="现在"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const p2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const hm = d => p2(d.getHours()) + ':' + p2(d.getMinutes());
let feeds = [];
let openIdx = -1;

function startOfToday() { const n = new Date(); return new Date(n.getFullYear(), n.getMonth(), n.getDate()); }

function save() { ducter.set('feeds', feeds); }

function render() {
  const start = startOfToday();
  const todays = feeds
    .map((f, i) => ({ ...f, i, d: new Date(f.t) }))
    .filter(f => f.d >= start)
    .sort …      if (!ml) return;
        feeds[f.i] = { t: t.toISOString(), ml };
        openIdx = -1; save(); render();
      };
      ed.querySelector('#e-del').onclick = () => {
        feeds.splice(f.i, 1); openIdx = -1; save(); render();
      };
    }
  });
  ducter.summary(todays.length ? todays.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + total + ' ml' : '今天还没记');
}

function parseTime(s) {
  const m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec((s || '').trim());
  if (!m) return null;
  const d = startOfToday();
  d.setHours(+m[1], +m[2], 0, 0);
  return d;
}

const chips = [60, 80, 90, 100, 120, 150];
chips.forEach(v => {
  const b = document.createElement('button');
  b.className = 'k-chip';
  b.textContent = v + ' ml';
  b.onclick = () => { $('ml').value = v; };
  $('chips').appendChild(b);
});

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if (!ml) return;
  const d = parseTime($('tm').value) || new Date();
  feeds.push({ t: d.toISOString(), ml });
  $('ml').value = ''; $('tm').value = '';
  $('add').closest('details').open = false;
  save(); render();
};

function load() {
  const v = ducter.get('feeds');
  feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"t":"2026-11-24T06:10:00+08:00","ml":120},{"t":"2026-11-24T09:05:00+08:00","ml":90}]}
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: "PRs waiting" is on your shelf with the four PRs, and ticking one off marks it landed and updates the count.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2383 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <span class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</span>
    <span class="k-sum-value" id="left">–</span>
    <span class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick a PR off when it lands</span>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row label { display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:flex-start; width:100%; cursor:pointer; }
  .k-row input[type=checkbox] { margin-top:4px; width:18px; height:18px; flex:none; accent-color:var(--d-accent); }
  .k-row.landed .k-row-main { text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--d-muted); }
  .k-row.landed .k-row-sub { color:var(--d-muted); }
</style>
<script>
  var DEFAULTS = ducter.get("defaults") || [];

  function prs() {
    var v = ducter.get('prs');
    return Array.isArray(v) && v.length ? v : DEFAULTS;
  }

  function save(list) { ducter.set('prs', list); }

  function render() {
    var list = prs();
    var ul = document.getElementById('list');
    ul.innerHTML = '';
    list.forEach(function (pr, i) {
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'k-row' + (pr.done ? ' landed' : '');

      var label = document.createElement('label');
      var cb = document.createElement('input');
      cb.type = 'checkbox';
      cb.checked = !!pr.done;
      cb.addEventListener('change', function () {
        var next = prs().slice();
        next[i] = Object.assign({}, next[i], {done: cb.checked});
        save(next);
      });

      var text = document.createElement('span');
      var main = document.createElement('span');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = pr.id + ' · ' + pr.title;
      var sub = document.createElement('span');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.style.display = 'block';
      sub.textContent = pr.who;
      text.appendChild(main);
      text.appendChild(sub);

      label.appendChild(cb);
      label.appendChild(text);
      li.appendChild(label);
      ul.appendChild(li);
    });

    var open = list.filter(function (p) { return !p.done; }).length;
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0
      ? 'All landed'
      : 'Tick a PR off when it lands';
    ducter.summary(open + ' open');
  }

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","who":"CI failing","done":false},{"id":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","who":"Draft, not yet asked for review","done":false}],"defaults":[{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","who":"CI failing","done":false},{"id":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","who":"Draft, not yet asked for review","done":false}]}
```

```
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
})();
```
